How do you find holy water?.

Spring water goes against purple, drinking water goes against blue and holy is white, thus, holy water will go
against white and purple and is value 8, being 7+1.

Blue is value 3 so ina town area where so much goes against white, being churches and everything that man
creates plus CC TV cables you have to out sort and fine code to trace the water lines to see how it becomes holy
so this code is 7+1+3, white, purple and blue. This code will over ride all other interference patterns.

that the plan of the water ways and wells formed an A and also seemed to match the oldest road plans into the
town which later were noted to be the same pattern as another well known place in the holy land.

By Roger Highfield
Sclenca Editor
FOR the first time a star has
been observed in real time as
it dies and becomes a super-
nova ~- an immensely power-
ful explosion. .

‘The cataclysmic supernova,
briefly outshining the. Milky
Way galaxy, occurred 440 miil-
lion light years away in a star-
forming galaxy in the constel-
lation of Aries.

Large stars are likely to end
their ‘days in this way,
blowing themselves to smith-
ereens. Aftermaths of super-
novae have been observed
many times but scientisis
have never before seen one of
the explosions happening.

They will now have an
unprecedented view of 2
supemova from start fo finish
across many wavelengths,
including radio and X-ray.

Radio telescopes have seen
the burst from the day it was
detected’ — another first. In a
few hundred years the super-
nova may tesemble. the wispy,
remnants of Cassiopeia A.

The event, described today
in the journal Nature, began
on Feb 18 with a garnma ray
burst (GRB), an unimaginably
powerful. outpouring of
gamma or X-rays. Astrono-
mers realised that it was an.
unusual event because it was
95 times closer and 100 times
longer than typical.

The burst,. called GRB
960218 after the daie it was
discovered, lasted almost 40
sninutes, rather than the more
usual few thousandths of a
second to tens of seconds.

The exceptionally loag
burst, in the form of a jet of
high-energy X-rays, pierced
through the doomed star from
its. core and sent out a warn-
ing within minutes thai a
supernova was imminent. As

by —

Scientists observe cataclysmic creation of a supernova for the first time

Desth thipes of a star begin, arrowed, as 8 supernova is born

the gamma rays faded’ away,
the massive star exploded.

Three telescopes on board
Nasa’s Swift satellite captured
the whole sequence. :

A‘ ‘ieam member, Paul
O’Brien, from. Leicester Uni-
versity, said: “The results sug-
gest that a broad jet expanded
into the surroundings. but it
was accompanied by a slower-
moving two million degree
bubble of gas produced from
the shock wave of the explod-
ing star. This is the first time
such en. extraordinary. event
has been seen from a GRB.”

The star’s. explosive power
is equivalent to 100,000,000,
060,000, 000,060, 000, 000,000,
000 tons of TNT, or to giving
off in half an hour as much
energy as our Sun radiates in
about a billion years.

Jobn Nousek, Swift's mis-
sion director at Penn State
University, Pennsylvania, said:
“There are still many
unknowns. This could be a
new kind of burst, or we may
be seeing a gamma-ray burst
from an entirely different

angle. This, off-angle glance
has given us an entirely new
approach to studying. star
explosions.” “

Swift's. telescopes - are
designed to cover gamma-ray,
K-ray, ultraviolet and optical

wavelengths of light. X-rays.
were seen to fade to ultravio-
jet then to the optical range.

Two days after the initial
explosion, the . ‘star had
become a classic supemove, a”
glowing cloud of ges powered
by the decay of adioactive
debris.

Prof Keith. Masoa, Britain's
jead -investigator’ for the
ultraviolet/optical telescope
on Swift, said: “Us aally these
evenis are not detected until
after the “supertova has.
brightened substentially in

2 optical wavele:gth, many
days after the expicsion.

“On this occasion we were
able to study the remarkable
event in all its glory from the
very beginning.” ;

Andrew Levan, of Hertford-
shire University, sa.d: “As well
as studying the early evolu-
tion of the supernova for. the
first time, these observations,
show how the material ejected
in the explosion evelves in the
following days and weeks.”

INTRODUCTION

When Section 4am to visit Tunbridge Weils with Sao: was formerly on the curator staff with
the Science Museum London to research a book on a historical matter, I took them on a walk around the town to

show them the sites, and while walking through the Grove I mentioned that the land had been given to the people
of Tunbridge Wells in about 1703 and I could not understand why building had at that time not been done both

sides of Buckingham Road,

Sect Ria AR: as it was a Grove it most likely was an old burial ground and that he would come down next and
dowse the site.

Little did we know what would be uncovered Sec ified an arrow head burial a major discovery and
while I watched him do the dowsing I noticed him working with a colour disk and he explained that the colours
acted as a witness to the matter being dowsed , working with about ten or so colours.

Later, as at timestwas an artist having a box full of acrylic paints I set about experimenting with the colours in a
dowsing fashion , hiaQht me how to dowse the graves he had traced, and the cbject was to find out
why a tribe 8500 years ago had laid out a burial of 129 persons in an arrow head formation directed to

Wellington Rocks .

When a set of colour reacted, Orange , White and Gold , which in itself was a totally different way of dowsing
with colour little did I know what these colour had unearthed and why . Later he e400 to the town

again we looked at the find in great detail and with others confirmed our find , which will remain sealed as to
what it was.

Ti has become plain why this area was called Andred Weald in ancient times, and in relation to what we were
later to discover the wording related to Druid and Androiode and in view of the latest press information that the
Americans in conjunction with others are to produce a human DNA micro silicon chip to have a human factor in
a computer.. How true this would be in relation to our later findings connecting to the past history of this town.

THE GROVE 1703/2003
Most are aware of the Groves history from 1703 to date, but what about the years prior to 1703.

Mary, daughter of Ulick De Burgh, 1 st Marquess of Clanricardi married charles Lord Muskerry who
died 2t sea in 1965. The estate of Somerhill at Tonbridge took In lands thal we now know as

Tunbridge Wells.

After the death of Lord Muskerry, Mary married Robert Feilding, a milMary man with a reputation,
known as Beau Feilding, or Major Feilding.

Feilding was married four times, firstly to Mary daughter of Barnham Swift, late created Viscount
Carlingford linking to the famous Johnathan Swift the writer. Next, hé married Mary De Burgh and
she died in 1898, at that point the land we know as the grove was in trust,

laior Feilding being short of money tried to trick a local widow Mrs Deleal inte marriage and asked
Mrs Deleeis hairdresser for assistance in the plot, but another lady Mary Wordsworth was iniroduced
to Beau Feilding who was himself tricked into marriage to the wrong lady on 8/41/7706. At the same
tirne Feilding wes also courting Barbara Villiers, the Duchess of Cleveland, the Duke Of
Buckinghams daughter and they married 25/14/1705,

The Matter came to court end Feilding was sent to Newgate, also for maltreating both of the ladies,
Darbara Villers marriage was nullified 2Y/S/1707 by Dectora Commons.

he Grove lands hac been in trust during the latter years some leases had been said off such as
outh Grove House, Middle Grove House, and North Grove House, the land between the Grove and
Srove Hil Road were meadow lands, the Grove was left in trust for the citizens of Tunbridge Wells,

H
‘gilding died 12/5/1772 age 81.

OD

My own family research ( The Elis Family), links to Beau Feilding via Barbara Villers who hac
married prior Sir Roger Palmer, later created Earl of Castlemain by King Charlee second in
recognition that Barbara had been the Kings mistress by whom he had a son, the Earl OF
Chesterfield, Sir Roger had firstly married an ancestor, one of the jour daughters of Thomas Elis
based in ireland where the family links to Tichbourne, Rochdale, and Viscounts Ciiiden through state
employments , what is interesting that the family tree further back in 1471 links another Thomas Elis
to Anne Calverley daughter of Walter Calverley which rather links the narne to other parts of present

Tunbridge Wells, the Celverley Park and House not far from the Grove .

There is no doubt that the Grove links to the Buckingham name and although major Feilding is said
to have been self styled Earl Of Buckingham, his brother in law was Robert Villers whos son John
was 2° Earl Of Buckingham.

Following reports land 2 I now issue report 3 which asks the question in respect to code 41, First Lask whom I

added so both groups were code 40 prior to the breeding to add the power of 6 which brings me to the evidence
from our own ways today we often quote things in threes , like three blind mice ,or three men in a boat or the

| am writing with reference to your e.mail on the 31 October 2007, to the MOD regarding who to contact to
report a UFO. This office is the focal point within the Ministry of Defence for correspondence relating to UFOs.

The sighting can be reported to one of the following:-

5!" Floor, Zone H, Main Building, Whitehall, London SW1A 2HB
Or - DAS answerphone number ~ 0207 218 2140

Or — By e.mail — das-ufo-office @mod.uk

Subject: Release-Authorised: Treat Official Correspondence: TO06588/2007

TREAT OFFICIAL CORRESPONDENCE - TO BE GIVEN A HIGH PRIORITY

To: DAS Sec

Copy To:

Our Reference: TO06588/2007

Due Date: ber 2007
Correspondent:

Additional Advice:

The Rt Hon Des Browne MP has received the attached correspondence from a member of
the public, which this office has neither retained nor acknowledged. Please send a reply on
behalf of the PM/Minister/Department.

Ministers attach great importance to correspondence being answered promptly, and your
reply should be sent within 15 working days of the date of this message. If,
exceptionally, this should prove impossible, an interim reply should be sent within the
same timescale. You should be aware that No 10 periodically calls for a sample of Jetters
sent by officials on the PM's behalf for his perusal.

If correspondence includes a specific request for recorded information then it should be
treated under the terms of the Freedom of Information Act 2000, even if the Act is not
specifically mentioned. In general, if you meet the Department's 15 working day deadline
and respond fully to the request for information, then there is no need to follow the full
procedures for FOI requests. However, you will still need to acknowledge that you have
applied the Act and provide details of their right of appeal (see link below). If the
correspondence requests information which is not already in the public domain, and
particularly if you considering withholding information, then you should formally treat it as
a FOI request. The correspondence should be logged on the Access to Information toolkit
and you should consult and comply with the separate FOI guidance from DG Info. Note, the
shorter deadline for responding to Ministerial and Treat Official correspondence will still
apply. If you are in any doubt as to whether a piece of correspondence should be treated
as an FOI request, you should ask your FOI Focal Point or refer to the guidance produced
by DG Info. (See the guidance at
http://defenceintranet.diiweb.r.mil.uk/DefenceIntranet/Admin/RespondToRequestsForInformatic

It is important that branches ensure they have simple systems to track
correspondence received from members of the public, though the Parliamentary
Toolkit records the basic details. If you have access to a DII/C terminal, please
follow this link (once a response has been sent) to add your Final Reply Date and
close the case to remove it from your TO Task List:
http://pt/_Layouts/PT/TaskList/TaskList.aspx. Lead Branches without access to

02/11/2007
«TREAT OFFICIAL CORRESPONDENCE - TO BE GIVEN A HIGH PRIORITY Page 2 of 2

he Toolkit should notify the Ministerial Correspondence Unit (via ParliBranch-
reat-Official@mod.uk) of the date of their reply so that Parli Branch can close
the record on the Toolkit.

Detailed guidance on handling TO Correspondence can be found on the Defence Intranet at
http://main.defence.mod.uk/min_parl/ParlBrch/TOGuid.htm. If you do not have access to
the Intranet, please inform the Ministerial Correspondence Unit.

Email: ParliBranch-Treat-Official@mod.uk

Regards,

MOD Parliamentary Branch
Ministerial Correspondence Unit

e: ParliBranch-Treat-Official@mod.uk

02/11/2007

Thank you for your letter dated 16 September 2007. Sorry for the delay in replying to you.

As stated before, the Ministry of Defence’s only purpose in looking at reports of ‘unexplained
aerial sightings’ — is to establish the presence of a matter which might be of defence concern, such
as unauthorised or hostile air activity in UK airspace. UK airspace is continually policed to ensure
no such aircraft enters our airspace.

Finally, with regards to your question on the matter of welcoming UFO sighting reports from
members of the public, whilst the MOD is happy to receive these reports, we are not funded to
research them all, which can cause disappointment for some. However, we keep an open mind and
publish details of the sightings on an annual basis.

I hope this is helpful.

Yours sincerely

Dex

Thank you for your e-mail of 19 September 2007 concerning the so called
Rendlesham Incident.

I think it may help if 1 explain that the Ministry of Defence examines any reports of ‘unidentified
flying objects’ it receives solely to establish whether what was seen might have some defence
significance; namely, whether there is any evidence that the United Kingdom's airspace might have
been compromised by hostile or unauthorized foreign military activity.

Unless there is evidence of a potential threat to the United Kingdom from an external military
source, and to date no 'UFO' report, (including the Rendlesham Incident) has revealed such evidence,
we do not attempt to identify the precise nature of each sighting reported to us. We believe that
rational explanations, such as aircraft lights or natural phenomena, could be found for them if
resources were diverted for this purpose, but it is not the function of the MOD to provide this kind of
aerial identification service. It would be an inappropriate use of defence resources if we were to do
so.

I believe that I outlined our position regarding this particular incident in my response to your
Freedom of Information request (06-09-2007-064728-002). Although the MoD remains open minded
on the subject of extra terrestrial life, I can only repeat that in the quarter of a century since this
alleged incident, nothing has emerged to make us believe that our original assessment was incorrect.

Dear AEN

Thank you for your Freedom of Information request asking for full disclosure on what
has become known as the Rendlesham Incident in 1980.

When the Ministry of Defence was informed of the events which are alleged to have occurred at
Rendlesham Forest/RAF Woodbridge in December 1980, all available substantiated evidence was
looked at in the usual manner by those within the MoD/RAF with responsibility for air defence
matters. The judgement was that there was no indication that a breach of the United Kingdom's air
defences had occurred on the nights in question. As there was no evidence to substantiate an event of
defence concern, no further investigation into the matter was necessary. Although a number of
allegations have subsequently been made about these reported events, nothing has emerged over the
last quarter of a century which has given us reason to believe that the original assessment made by
this Department was incorrect. The MoD file on the matter is available for viewing at www.mod.uk
by searching under the word Rendlesham.

If you are unhappy with this response or you wish to complain about any aspect of the handling of

your request, then you should contact me in the first instance. If informal resolution is not possible
and you are still dissatisfied then you may apply for an independent internal review by contacting the

20/09/2007
Page 2 of 2

eu“ of Information Exploitation, 6th Floor, MOD Main Building, Whitehall, SW1A 2HB (e-
ail Info-XD@mod.uk). Please note that any request for an internal review must be made within 40
working days of the date on which the attempt to reach informal resolution has come to an end.

If you remain unhappy following an internal review, you may take your complaint to the Information
Commissioner under the provisions of Section 50 of the Freedom of Information Act. Please note
that the Information Commissioner will not investigate the case until the internal review process has
been completed. Further details of the role and powers of the Information Commissioner can be
found on the Commissioner's website, http://www.informationcommissioner.gov.uk
<hitp://www.informationcommissioner.gov.uk/> ."

Correspondent: [Rinne ,

[*MinAddName*] has received the attached correspondence from a member of the public,
which this office has neither retained nor acknowledged. Please send a reply on behalf of
the PM/Minister/Department.

Ministers attach great importance to correspondence being answered promptly, and your
reply should be sent within 15 working days of the date of this message. If,
exceptionally, this should prove impossible, an interim reply should be sent within the
same timescale. You should be aware that No 10 periodically calls for a sample of letters
sent by officials on the PM's behalf for his perusal.

Most correspondence involves some form of request for information - even if it is only a
request for clarification of Government policy - and is therefore covered by the Freedom of
Information Act (FOIA) from January 2005. In general, if you meet the deadline for
responding to correspondence, and comply with any requests for information, there is no
need to do anything differently as this will meet the requirements of the Act. However, if
the correspondence requests information which is not already in the public domain, and
which might need to be withheld, then you should treat it as a FOIA request, track it using
the Access to Information toolkit, and comply with the separate FOI guidance from DG Info
(see http://aitportal/default.aspx for details). However, the deadline for responding to
correspondence will still apply. If you are in any doubt as to whether a piece of
correspondence should be treated as an FOIA request, you should ask your FOI Focal Point
or refer to the guidance produced by DG Info.

It Is important that branches ensure they have simple systems to track
correspondence received from members of the public, though the Parliamentary
Toolkit will now record the basic details. Once a response has been sent,
therefore, can you please notify the Ministerial Correspondence Unit (via
ParliBranch-Treat-Official@mod.uk) of the date of your reply so that the record
can be closed on the Toolkit.

Detailed guidance on handling TO Correspondence can be found on the Defence Intranet at
http://main.defence.mod.uk/min_parl/ParlBrch/TOGuid.htm. If you do not have access to
the Intranet, please inform the Ministerial Correspondence Unit.

NEWS 31

Scientists put new spin on Earth's core

By Mark Henderson
Science Correspondent

THE Earth’s inner core is rotat-
ing faster than the rest of the
planet, scientists have found.

An ingenious comparison of
earth es has revealed that
the sofid ball of iron and nickel
at the’ centre of the planet is
spinning about half a degree
more quickly than its upper lay-
ers. While the excess spin is
oer. small, it means that over_

years the very centre of the
Earth will complete one more
revolution than the rest.

The findings settle a scientif-
ic controversy that has raged
for nine years, since Professor
Paul Richards, of Columbia
University in New York, and
Professor Xiaodong Song, of
the University of Illinois at
Urbana-Champaign, first pro-

_ posed the core’s odd behaviour.

at

Their 1996 study was widely
disputed, with many research-
ers suggesting that the appar-
ent extra spin was in fact
caused by measurement errors.

While the core is poorly un-
derstood — the great physicist

Richard Feynman once said we

- know more about the centre of

the Sun than the centre of the
Earth — it is critical to the
make-up of the’ planet, and
indeed to life on Earth. Cur-
rents in the core create a vast
dynamo that generates the

planet’s magnetic field. With-
out this, the surface would be
exposed to. radioactive cosmic
rays and plasma from solar
flares that would make the ex-
istence of complex life all but
impossible.

In the new study, details of
which are published in the
journal Science, a team led by
Professor Song and Professor
Richards made use of earth-
quakes in the South Sandwich
Islands, in the Pacific Ocean.

Two earthquakes, in 1993
and 2003, occurred no more
thah half a mile apart and gen-
erated precisely the same pat-
terns of peaks and troughs on
seismographs’ thousands of
miles away in Alaska. The seis-
mic waves from the 2003 earth-
quake, which passed through
the Earth’s core, arrived at the
Alaska monitoring. station
about a tenth of a second more
guickly than they did in 1993.

ly an inner core spinning
faster than other parts of the
Earth can explain this.

The most likely explanation
is electromagnetic coupling,
the phenomenon that drives
electric motors. “The magnetic
field generated in the outer
core diffuses into the inner
core, where it generates an elec-
trie current,” Professor Song
said. “The interaction of that
current with the magnetic field
causes the inner core to spin.”

Seismic waves from
the earthquakes
pass through
Earth’s core

% A MASSIVE diamond of 10billion-
trillion trillion carats has been found in
deep space.

The 2,500-mile wide star made from
crystallised carbon was discovered in
the constellation Centaurus about 50
light years from the Earth.

“You would need a jeweller’s loupe as
big as the Sun to grade this diamond,’
said astronomer Travis Metcalfe.

The star has been named Lucy, after
the Beatles song Lucy In The Sky

- With Diamonds. .

Once as bright as our Sun, Lucy is all

that is left of an old star which used up
- . all its nuclear fuel and died.- :

Astronomers predict our Sun will
become a white dwarf when it dies in
an estimated Sbillion years.

Lucy is not only radiant but also
pulsates like a gigantic gong.

Mr Metcalfe and a team at Harvard-
Smithsonian Center for Astrophysics
have measured these pulsations.

“As a result, We figured out the car-
bon interior of this white dwarf has

: ’ solidified to form the galaxy’s largest

diamond,’ Mr Metcalfe added. -

ecycle

correction.

> aT

I am writing regarding your answerphone message, with regard to details of your various sightings
of UFOs and UFO phenomena.

You will know our policy on UFOs, due to my previous correspondence for which I wrote to you
on the 7 August 2007.

With regard to your particular observations, I am not disputing the fact that you witnessed UFOs,
just that the Ministry of Defence (MOD), has no interest or role in respect to ‘UFO/flying saucer’
matters, or to the question of the existence or otherwise of extraterrestrial life forms about which it
remains totally open-minded. To date, however, the MOD is unaware of any evidence that proves
that these phenomena exist. The MOD examines any reports of ‘UFO’ sightings it receives solely
to establish whether what was seen might have some defence significance; namely, if there is any
evidence that the UK’s airspace might have been compromised by hostile or unauthorised air
activity. UK Airspace is continually policed to ensure that no such aircraft enters our airspace.

I hope this is helpful and explains our policy clearly.

Thank you for your second answerphone message, citing more details of your sighting on the 6
August 2007, for which have been added to your previous report.

You will know our policy due to my previous letter, for which I wrote to you on the 6 August
2007.

The Ministry of Defence’s only purpose in looking at reports of ‘unexplained aerial sightings’ — is
to establish the presence of a matter which might be of defence concern, such as unauthorised or
hostile aircraft in UK Airspace. UK Airspace is continually policed to ensure that no such aircraft
enters our airspace.

Also, as stated before, the MOD does not have any expertise or role in respect of ‘UFO/flying
saucer’ matters to the question of the existence or otherwise of extraterrestrial life forms, about
which it remains totally open-minded. I should add that to date, the MOD knows of no evidence
which substantiates the existence of these alleged phenomena.

Finally, sorry, that I could not give you an explanation as to what you saw.

Section 4q 7 August 2007

Dear NE

Thank you for your letter dated 7 July 2007 for which I have just received.

First, it may be helpful if I explain that the Ministry of Defence examines any reports of
‘unidentified flying objects’ it receives solely to establish whether what was seen might have some
defence significance; namely, whether there is any evidence that the United Kingdom’s airspace
might have been compromised by hostile or unauthorised air activity. Unless there is evidence of a
potential threat to the United Kingdom from an external source, and to date no ‘UFO’ report has
revealed such evidence, we do not attempt to identify the precise nature of each sighting reported
to us. We believe that rational explanations, such as aircraft lights or natural phenomena, could be
found for them if resources were diverted for this purpose, but it is not the function of the MOD to
provide this kind of aerial identification service. It would be an inappropriate use of defence
resources if we were to do so.

The MOD does not have any expertise or role in respect of ‘UFO/flying saucer’ matters to the
question of the existence or otherwise of extraterrestrial life forms, about which it remains totally
open-minded. I should add that to date, the MOD knows of no evidence which substantiates the
existence of these alleged phenomena.

Sorry I could not be any help.

Finally, your comments have been noted and your letter will be placed on our files.

Yours sincerely

The following is a transcript of a letter written by!

In 1983 I gave up a vicious heroin, valium and cocaine habit and became a marathon
runner. I even managed to stop smoking cigarettes and beer and all I did for three
years was run 16 miles every night after a full days work as a commodity broker, 26
miles every Saturday and partook in 2 London marathons finishing in 3 '? hours and
a triathlon, swim a mile, bicycle 32 miles and run 11 miles.

I was on top of he world - I had only ever had one admission to the Charter clinic in
London for 7 days and that was because I was going out with a lady called Section #@

1#Dhad claimed to have met people from another planet and one day she
walked into my flat with a cross in her uterus and I went crazy and ripped the house to
pieces. After a while I got scared of investigating her and her crazy friends mixing
with UFO people because there was too much black magic involved. All I can tell
you is there is a planet where their beings have red eyes and they are totally demonic.
I have spoken to a cousin of mine, Sir Desmond Leslie from Castle Leslie in Ireland,
he wrote the book “UFO’s are here” with George Adamski which went into
publication 14 times in the States. He implied on the telephone to me that there was
one group of people who were really nasty and evil in the UFO world and Desmond
advises the military on UFO’s. he is a good friend of Patrick Moore and you can
always get hold of him in the phone book. Paul McCartney did, he got married at
Castle Leslie in Ireland. I am not going to go into the story of how I came across a
batch of people who had something to do with people from another planet because it
is irrelevant to my story but I will gladly tell you everything if you take the trouble to
interview me.

By March of 1986 I had got rid of this ERAN woman and was no longer
interested in investigating UFO’s. I was back to working out in the gym everyday and
marathon running. My sister at the time was engaged to a Film Director who had a
house in Hollywood Hills in L.A. and they invited me to go and stay there. I didn’t
really want to go, I wanted a windsurfing holiday in Jamaica but so as not to offend
my sister and her husband to be EMME | decided to go.

When I touched down in L.A. it was just what I didn’t want to see. Too many cars,
bad weather even though it was spring- so no wind surfing and there was something
in the air I couldn’t quite put my hand on. It was in fact a UFO with a circumference
of about 3 miles and a radius of about 1.5 miles. You couldn’t see the UFO- it was
about the size of the ones in Independence day- don’t forget this was 1986 and that
film didn’t come out for another 8 years 1994- also if you check my mental health
records you will see I’ve been saying the same thing for 21 years. You could feel the
UFO and from what I’ve been told by Desmond they travel along the San Andreas
fault line using the centre of the earth for some kind of energy like solar power that
they have tapped into. I spent two weeks in L.A. running marathon, going to the
beach and going to the running track. I didn’t use any drugs but I had a gram or 2 of
cocaine 2 weeks before. However, later when I had my accident no trace of cocaine
was found in my blood supply or piss.
Finally on 1* April 1986 I was so possessed by this UFO that I got angry broke my
brother in law’s sun glasses on purpose because I know he was very psychic and I
wanted him to see this invisible energy hovering above L.A. He didn’t see it, he got
angry and he banished me from the house for a week telling me to drive to Vegas. I
had rented a car so I left.

I drove out of town- it was very hard to describe what happened next but I will try- I
was very in love at the time with a girl in England who was about to fly over to L.A.,
she too was in the film business. Basically I drove the car up to some barren pieces of
waste ground on a sand track and got the wheels caught in the sand. I was in a kind of
trance and put my accelerator flat down to try and get out of the rut the car was stuck
in- to cut a long story short the engine of the car caught fire and the petrol pipe
beneath the dashboard burst open and a whole load of burning petrol fell onto my foot
burning it really badly- I jumped out of the car and was so dazed that I left the car
burning and started walking off into the undergrowth of the Californian desert. It was
about 11am by 11pm I had walked about 30 miles-I was stark naked because I had
tried to put the fire out with my clothes and I was standing by a tree having a vision of
the Virgin Mary, Joseph and Jesus. All I can tell you is the love they had for each
other was so powerful so mind blowingly beautiful, it was unbelievable. I forgot to
mention that as well as all my keep fit classes I was going to the Brompton oratory
Catholic services every morning and I was undergoing instruction to change from
being a protestant to a Catholic.

While I was standing by this tree there were two other beings wearing suits with lights
coming up onto their faces which were covered by glass bowls. I could only see the
face of one and he had short black hair and was actually very good looking; but I
could see from his skin he was humanoid and he wasn’t really human. He physically
told me to stay where I was — on his back he had some kind of tank and he was
holding like a weed sprayer in his hand spraying this white gas all over the valley- the
other alien was doing the same. The gas looked like dry ice and I was thinking that
all these aliens had to do was love Joseph, Jesus and Mary like me and they would be
ok. While this was going on there was a massive UFO as I said, a mile in diameter at
least flying round in a circle above me and no longer not visible. It seemed to be
pulling along a cloud and it had about 20 craft flying off it. Red, blue and white lights
glimmering on and off. You could see they were not aircraft because they stuck to no
particular flight pattern and were flying in opposite directions about 50 feet away
from each other which is illegal for commercial flight craft.

After hours of watching all this I legged it back to L.A. I ran naked through 30 miles
of gorse cutting my feet up really badly and eventually made it to Cedars Sinai
Hospital. I was there for about 10 days at least on a penicillin drip feed. My wrists
were split open along with my head and my body was covered in cuts and bruises.
The worst thing of all was this white gas had got inside the burn of my foot and my
whole leg had swollen up terribly.

While I was in hospital, something very interesting came over the news. The Police
thought that a taxi driver, his wife and 3 children had been kidnapped by the mafia
because their house had been beaten up; but it emerged on the L.A news channel that
they had gone into some kind of trance and beaten up their own house. They had also
driven out into the wasteland like I had and seen all the UFO stuff I had. They had no
mental or criminal history and you could still trace them today by checking out the
local news channel in L.A from the 1“ April — 10 April 1986. they said what they
saw, they couldn’t find words to describe and even if they could , no one would
believe them;

When I returned to England I wanted to believe I had imagined the whole scenario but
suddenly I realised J couldn’t run more than 5 miles at a time- I couldn’t work for
more than % an hour and I had a hankering to smoke cigarettes again. I suddenly
started to have major mental break downs night and day. I realised I had some kind of
reptilian life force living in cell form in my body. The white smoke that got into my
leg I talked about earlier was some kind of seed, like human sperm, but it was
reptilian and it had given birth to an energy which was possessing me terribly. Twice
I have nearly burst into flames and my whole body become ashes sending my soul to
hell and you wouldn’t believe the physical pain and discomfort J live in. I only have a
criminal record for stealing a petrol siphon from a shop aged 19 after my mother had
died and a £20 bag of heroin both in 1979. 25 years later I am treated like a rapist.

From:

pection
Text as discussed.

The Ministry of Defence plans to open its "X-Files" on UFO sightings to the public for
the first time. Officials have not yet decided on a date for the release of the
reports, which date back to 1967, but it is hoped to be within weeks.

The move follows the decision by the French national space agency to release its UFO
files in March, the first official body in the world to do so.

UFO buffs will be keen to find out what. officials knew about some of the UK's most
famous sightings and whether any action was taken. One celebrated event - at .
Rendlesham Forest, Suffolk, in 1980 - has been dubbed "Britain's Roswell" after the
UFO incident in the US in 1947. At Rendlesham there were several witness reports of a
UFO apparently landing. The released files should support or discount claims that
radiation was detected at the site after the event.

David Clarke, a lecturer in journalism at Sheffield Hallam University and author of
Flying Saucerers: A Social History of UFOlogy, said opening the MoD's files would make
it harder to sustain the idea that evidence for the existence of aliens has been
suppressed. "The more of this stuff that they put on their website or put in the
national archives, the less it will cost the taxpayer, because at the moment people
are writing in about individual incidents and they are having respond," said Dr
Clarke, referring to requests under the Freedom of Information Act.

The documents due for release are witness reports of apparent UFO sightings, many by
civil pilots and military personnel. Most were simply collected-and filed by a small,
secret unit within defence intelligence called DI55. A few are thought to have been
investigated further by the military, but the details have never been made public.
There are 24 files due for release, each containing 200-300 reports of sightings, plus
internal MoD briefings and correspondence.

TREAT OFFICIAL CORRESPONDENCE - TO BE GIVEN A HIGH PRIORITY

To: DAS Sec
Copy To:

Our Reference: T003044/2007
Due Date: 06 June 2007

the Department has received the attached correspondence from a member of the public,
which this office has neither retained nor acknowledged. Please send a reply on behalf of
the PM/Minister/Department.

Ministers attach great importance to correspondence being answered promptly, and your
reply should be sent within 15 working days of the date of this message. If,
exceptionally, this should prove impossible, an interim reply should be sent within the
same timescale. You should be aware that No 10 periodically calls for a sample of letters
sent by officials on the PM's behalf for his perusal.

Most correspondence involves some form of request for information - even if it is only a
request for clarification of Government policy - and is therefore covered by the Freedom of
Information Act (FOIA) from January 2005. in general, if you meet the deadline for
responding to correspondence, and comply with any requests for information, there is no
need to do anything differently as this will meet the requirements of the Act. However, if
the correspondence requests information which is not already in the public domain, and
which might need to be withheld, then you should treat it as a FOIA request, track it using
the Access to Information toolkit, and comply with the separate FOI guidance from DG Info
(see http://aitportal/default.aspx for details). However, the deadline for responding to
correspondence will still apply. If you are in any doubt as to whether a piece of
correspondence should be treated as an FOIA request, you should ask your FOI Focal Point
or refer to the guidance produced by DG Info.

Thank you for your letter of 9 April 2007 asking if there was any military aircraft activity in the
Cullen Bay area of Banffshire on 24!" March 2007 at 13:30 hours.

The Ministry of Defence does not keep a central record of aircraft flights or activity and therefore we are
unable to answer your question. However, this area of air space would fall under the control of the civilian air
traffic control at Edinburgh who would direct the movement of air traffic (including military aircraft) in that area
and it is possible that they may have a record of aircraft present at the time.

lam sorry that | could not be more helpful.

Thank you for your e-mail of 13 March 2007 to Ministers regarding the fitting of
aircraft with cameras and the reporting off any incidents involving foreign activity. It has been
passed to this office to answer.

Firstly, I should explain that the integrity of the UK's airspace in peacetime is maintained through
continuous surveillance of the UK Air Policing Area by the Royal Air Force. This is achieved by
using a combination of civil and military radar installations, which provide a continuous real-time
“picture” of the UK airspace. Any threat to the UK Air Defence Region would be handled in the
light of the particular circumstances at the time (it might if deemed appropriate, involve the
scrambling or diversion of air defence aircraft).

You may be interested to know that the RAF’s fast jet aircraft are already fitted with Head Up
Display cameras as standard.

I hope this is helpful.

Yours sincerely,

TREAT OFFICIAL CORRESPONDENCE - TO BE GIVEN A HIGH PRIORITY

To: DAS Sec

Copy To: HQ STC/PTC Sec,
Our Reference: T001721/2007
Due Date: 13 April 2007

The Rt Hon Des Browne MP has received the attached correspondence from a member of
the public, which this office has neither retained nor acknowledged. Please send a reply on
behalf of the PM/Minister/Department.

Ministers attach great importance to correspondence being answered promptly, and your
reply should be sent within 15 working days of the date of this message. If,
exceptionally, this should prove impossible, an interim reply should be sent within the
same timescale. You should be aware that No 10 periodically calls for a sample of letters
sent by officials on the PM's behalf for his perusal.

Most correspondence involves some form of request for information - even if it is only a
request for clarification of Government policy - and is therefore covered by the Freedom of
Information Act (FOIA) from January 2005. In general, if you meet the deadline for
responding to correspondence, and comply with any requests for information, there is no
need to do anything differently as this will meet the requirements of the Act. However, if
the correspondence requests information which is not already in the public domain, and
which might need to be withheld, then you should treat it as a FOIA request, track it using
the Access to Information toolkit, and comply with the separate FOI guidance from DG Info
(see http://aitportal/default.aspx for details). However, the deadline for responding to
correspondence will still apply. If you are in any doubt as to whether a piece of
correspondence should be treated as an FOIA request, you should ask your FOI Focal Point
or refer to the guidance produced by DG Info.

It is important that branches ensure they have simple systems to track
correspondence received from members of the public, though the Parliamentary
Toolkit will now record the basic details. Once a response has been sent,
therefore, can you please notify the Ministerial Correspondence Unit (via
ParliBranch-Treat-Official@mod.uk) of the date of your reply so that the record
can be closed on the Toolkit.

Detailed guidance on handling TO Correspondence can be found on the Defence Intranet at

A memorable/frightening sighting took place on
October 28th, 1980, between 4.40 and 4.55 pm.

After observing a bright star like object almost
directly overhead, to high an elevation to be an
evening star, a strange event took place over South
London, A black corkscrewing plume almost to
feint to see at first could be seen dropping below
the ‘star’ which remained in place. This black trail
was observed falling for over ten minutes.

When the object causing the trail reached about
30,000 ft it divided into three, still leaving trails,
which fell levelling out at about a 1,000ft and these
objects then flew towards the horizon disappearing
to the SE, S, SW. The ‘star’ stayed directly over-
head throughout and the whole event took about
15 minutes. About this time tapes recording radar
contacts were removed by the MOD from RAF
Neatishead this again happened after the Rendle-
sham Forest incident in the following
December/January.

SNCOUNTER AT 35,000 ft

Travelling back from Majorca (March 15) on Flight 344B from Palma
having just passed over the Pyrenees about GMT 10.25 (my watch). I
was looking out of the window right side when I saw a vapour trail
diving straight down a few hundred yards away, it was almost black but
golden coloured on the sun side. The odd thing apart from being close
to the plane and diving was that the bottom of the trail was almost level
with the aircraft. Although I told my wife it was a meteorite which was
my first impression because of its closeness to the plane and I could not
see a cause. Then it became apparent that our plane must have been
tracking it because if it was dropping/diving I should have lost sight of
the bottom of the trail, but we observed it for about 112 minutes through
the same window (I used clouds to give perspective and orientation),
eventually the contrail angled and levelled out and a small black object
flew back behind the plane (still level) until out of sight. Because of its
size another aircraft should be ruled out untess it was a small jet trainer
doing a massive loop the loop.

I can assure you it was close enough to be a near miss if it was a craft,
but as I say our plane seemed to be tracking with it with no sudden
movements. Its size when seen was small, car size, definitely not big-
ger than the Jet Trainer.

T E IN

Bizarre theory that-connects Taken, ET and Rendlesham Forest. : oe
Speilberg used Taken to link all of the major UFO incidents in America from foo fighter’s right up to the
Flying Triangle being escorted, all reported sightings covered by UFO over the years. The child character
central to the plot is Allie Keys whose family is joined with the alien race and pursued by the Crawford’s,
That lovable ET spends his time on Earth causing fun and mayhem but also trying (unsuccessfully) to avoid
being captured. The story of ET is in two parts that are interwoven, Speilberg wanted to make a film about a
child from a single parent family who has an alien friend, using screen writers to enlarge the idea he makes
ET, which came out in 1982. The other part of the story is about a man’s obsession to capture an alien. This
man was a shadowy government agent with the nickname of Keys, there are two reasons for this: first, he
carries a large bunch of keys on his belt which jangle when he walks, second, he is known as a bloke who
can open a lot of doors. Connection; Keys,

The link to Rendlesham is in the screenplay descriptions of events involving ET’s arrival and departure from
Earth could almost come from statements and stories of the people involved at Woodbridge.

These descriptions from both include the ufo looking like a Christmas Tree, also a mist being used to hide
the craft. The ship giving off a Lavender Blue light and a rainbow of colours. Also, there is mention of a
plaster cast being made and an analysis of fuel emissions. A strong point is made of well armed men run-
ning through the forest with torches. It has been suggested that armed soldiers unintentionally took their
weapons into the forest at the first sighting in Rendlesham. Also the confusion is mentioned in both ac-
counts. Add the barking dog and the usually quite log/fire road and the two stories almost mirror each other.
I’m not suggesting ET is a true story but that, perhaps, somebody when making the film asked the American
Air Force for help when writing the script. President Reagan is known to have said at a private showing of
ET: “There are about seven people in this room who know that everything in this movie is absolutely true”.
Of Rendlesham a Prime Minister said: “You can’t tell the people”.

Another link to Rendlesham is in the film Close Encounters of the Third Kind the opening sighting is similar
to the original sighting in 1956 of the Bentwater?Lakenheath event.

‘Three’s a crowd...

AT 11.03 am on April 23 rd, 2004, while looking west
from Bromley at a large plane, banking and climbing
from Heathrow, two very bright objects could be seen
above and behind .it. The reason they were worth
watching was that they were very reflective and slow
moving.

The brightest and largest object slowly made its
way easterly, looking as if it was tumbling or rotating.
Directly overhead the object gave off a star shape
flare from the sun’s reflection but seemed to have a
solid coffin type shape. The other object was in its
original position more or less, it was either way be-
hind, which made it look smaller, or a lot higher still.
It made small progress easterly, this whole event took
about % hour. Five minutes later, scanning the skies
again, a new object, or the back of one of the original
two, was observed in the east. This one was slightly
oval, black in colour (shadow) with a slight red tinge
on the right side, Lost sight due to rooftops and trees.
These objects were not basket type hot air balloons,
although they were high enough for weather balloons.
They were flying over Heathrow and Gatwick
flightpaths.

From: <customercentre@metoffice.com> Sub-
ject: RE: balloons Date: 07 May 2004 05:45
Thank you for your enquiry requesting infarma-
tion on weather balloons.
Basically what you have found is a weather
balloon, also know as a radiosonde. These are
launched front several focations around the UK
and rest of the world around 4 times per day.
The nearest station to the north of London is
Notting!

8 elu

box containing weather instruments (a radio-
‘sonde) attached to them. When the balloon Is
launched it can reach heights of 25,000 m and
travet long distances. As the bailoon rises,
pressure decreases and tha balioon keeps ex-
panding, until eventually it bursts, and the para-
chute aliows it to fail to the ground gently.
During its flight the balloon is tracked by radar
to measure wind speed and direction. The in-
struments in the radiosonde send beck mea-
surements of temperature, pressure and
toisture in the atmosphere, Kind Regards Mike

EASTANGLIA

HE mystery of Rendlesham

Forest probably goes back to

the mid-twelfth century.
A corridor that stretches from the
East coast at Bentwaters to Laken-
heath has probably he most docu-
mented sightings that date back
nearly a thousand years, Strangely,
the main stories are very similar.
There are three very similar encoun-
ters which share the same locations
in East Anglia, each with similar
descriptions and a military presence
to record the events. In 1980 in
Rendlesham Forest American air-
man find an unusual craft in the
woods outside an operational air-
base, this eventually takes off and
appears to split into three and two
parts appear merge in another part
of he sky. Different shapes and col-
ours are noted and distances of
around 10 miles from the base are
given for some of the sightings.
Going back to 1956 and there was
another encounter known as the
Lakenheath sighting. At Bentwa-
ters a radar contact of a craft travel-
ling very fast and passing over the
base and moving towards Laken-
heath where it was also observed
visually. An RAF jet was guided to
its position several times in pursuit.
Again, we have sightings of three

different shapes and colours per-
forming amazing aerobatics, some
merging to form one contact. Amer-
ican airmen and RAF personnel
were involved in this sighting.

In 1646, again in the same area,
there are reports of three strange
objects like fiery swords performing
amazing aerobatics merging and
separating above the heads of army
observers and good citizens. Three
stories that are almost identical,
could they be a phenomena that ap-
pears in skies above East Anglia at
irregular intervals.

Other strange stories from this area
are the Green Children of Woolpit,
who were found wandering in the
forest, their skin was green in colour
and they spoke a strange language
and the Merman of Orford caught
in fishermen’s nets, an extremely
hairy man who could not communi-
cate in a known tongue. These sto-
ries come from the Twelfth century.
The 1577 Black Dog of Bungay
entered two churches in Bungay and
Blythburg killing two people, leav-
ing one badly burned and scorched
scratch-marks can still be seen on
the door of one of the churches.

*XEVIN CONDE fae""

This is a letter that I sent to Michael Hanlon at the Daily Mail, July 3, 2003 in response to his recent de-
bunking of the Rendtesham Event,

With reference to your article of reporting the confession of Kevin Conde. Although I do not dispute that he
did perpetrate a hoax around that time I cannot believe his hoax is the same event known as the Rendlesham
Forest Mystery. For a start if he was on duty that night he would have been interviewed by any inquiry USAF
or MOD and for some reason he does not appear to have been missed from his post, meaning he must have
colluded with a partner to have pulled it off. He also didn’t own up when it was realised that an inquiry would
take place.

The car that was used is described as a battered 1979 Plymouth Volare as the event happened in 1980 the
vehicle was just over a year old and presumably only driven on base hardly the place to wreck a brand new
car, A bit of embellishment?

If Mr. Conde was unaware of the “Rendlesham Mystery” how come he says that “as far as he was aware
they found nothing above background (radiation) levels”?

Ifa bit of ordinary factfinding had been done it would have shown the event happened off base in the forest
not on the taxiway which meant the American servicemen left the base some possibly still carrying weapons
they did not have time to hand in (assuming they were running to help a downed aircraft). If a helicopter was
responsible for the indentations the night before then it would have been remembered for being the same
spot, surely, by somebody. As this was in the forest and tree branches were broken above the site it would
have been a major incident in its own right.

If Michael Hanlon wishes to be a debunker I would advise him to read Miss Bruni’s book and also UFO
Magazine, but perhaps even better, divulge who rattled his chain, to write a full page article with so little to
say, on evidence that is flimsy to say the least, Also he might like to check up on the Lakenheath sighting in
1956 which also is weli documented by the RAF and USAF when an unidentified object actually flew over
the same base at Rendlesham before being chased by RAF fighters and tracked on American radar—ERIC
RUSH : :

Dear Mr Rush.

Thank you for your letter. You make several points which deserve an answer.

1) As far as I know, Mr Conde was not AWOL in any sense; his duties. involved driving around the perimeter
of the base. No one would have ‘missed’ him because he was not supposed to be in any particular place at any
particular time.

2) He has been checking out the 'mystery’ for some months now, and told me he has indeed looked into the
radiation levels findings, It has become accepted myth that these levels were "far above background" which
is, indeed what Col Halt says in his memo. I understand however that this may have been a mistake.

3) Helicopters land in forest clearings all the time.

4) Ms Bruni is a Believer. Her book maintains that a UFO visited Rendlesham at this time. 1 do not believe
that this is so, and so I am unlikely to learn anything from her book.

5) The car - fair point. Conde described it to me as "one of those old Plymouth Volares" and, foolishly, I didn't
stop to think .... However, judging by the woeful standards of US automobile construction in the 1970s, I can
quite believe even a one-year-old General Motors product would have been quite battered and beaten up ...
This all boils down not so much to what you believe, but to what you want to believe. Some people seem to
have a desperate need to believe that the Earth is regularly visited by atiens from outer space, just as the same
sort of people believed in visitations by angels in the old days. I have no idea why this should be so. I suppose
it makes the world a more interesting place for these people. | find the whole UFO phenomenon fascinating,
not because it says anything interesting about what is Out There, but for what it says about what is In Here.
The psychology of ‘alien abductions’ is particularly bizarre. For the record, I believe that there probably are
several alien civilisations out there (although the number may have been overstated) but I think it is
inconceiveable that any would have chosen to visit us, Yet. This may change, of course, as our presence in
the cosmos becomes ever more advertised. But if They do come and visit, I think it unlikely that their chosen
point of contact would be a load of bored US airmen in a lonely corner of Suffolk ... ..

Best wishes, Mike Hanlon .

Thank you for your prompt reply. I feel aa need to qualify myself though I
am not certain why.

I am sending an attachment you might wish to add to my file. It shows my ability to,
among other things, notice things that other people don't and for those things to be
both valid and important.

This attachment as well as the other attachments I have sent show my ability to
analyze both photo and print for not only patterns that other miss but simultaneously
inconsistencies (such as in satellite photos). It's a Buddhist way of seeing things.
(I have been a Nyingma Tibetan Buddhist for 26 years.) It's a type of training in
which you learn to see what is there by learning how to avoid seeing that which you
want to see. Briefly we learn, often with a tv remote control, to only look at what we
want to see and to turn off (to) what we don't want to see or that which is
unfamiliar. I learned to do the opposite. It is not hard it just takes a long time to
learn to do.

In this photograph

<http: //www.globalsecurity.org/military/world/iraq/images/dg-

tharthar2.jpg> almost everyone sees the 'fun' amusement park rides (which is what they
want to see) where as I see the launchpads under the rides (which are not at all

‘fun' to see). By the way they are too far apart (at about 50 meters) for rides but
not for ballistic missiles. For them they are just about perfectly spaced.

Again I thank you very much.

@ Wh wrote the Sonnets attributed to Shakespeare?

Researchers often try to determine the personality of the Bard by the most personal writings
attributed to Shakespeare. It never leads anywhere unless you consider confusion as a valid
destination.

The reason is simple. There was more than one writer involved. Just take a look at the number of
uses of the word 'time'. The word time is used 86 times in the first 128 sonnets and not once in the 26
remaining sonnets (of 154 total).

thee.
Tl.

When forty
winters shall
beseige thy
brow,

And dig deep
trenches in thy
beauty's field,
Thy youth's
proud livery, so
gazed on now,
Will bea
tatter'd weed,
of small worth
held:

Then being
ask'd where all
thy beauty lies,
‘Where all the
treasure of thy
lusty days,

To say, within
thine own
deep-sunken
eyes,

Were an all-
eating shame
and thriftless
praise.

How much
more praise
deserved thy
beauty’s use,
If thou couldst
answer ‘This
fair child of
mine

Shall sum my
count and
make my old
excuse,’
Proving his
‘beauty by
succession
thine!

This were to be
new made
when thou art
old,

And see thy
blood warm
when thou
feel'st it cold.

TH.

Look in thy
glass, and tell,
the face thou
viewest

Now is the

time tat
face should
form another;
Whose fresh
repair if now
thou not
renewest,

Thou dost
beguile the
world, unbless
some mother.
For where is
she so fair
whose unear'd
womb
Disdains the
tillage of thy
husbandry?

Or who is he so
fond will be the

fair,

Can make you
live yourself in
eyes of men,
To give away
yourself keeps
yourself still,
And you must
live, drawn by
your own sweet
skill.

XVH,

Who will
believe my
verse in

:
time
come,

If it were fill'd
with your most
high deserts?
Though yet,
heaven knows,
itis but asa
tomb

Which hides
your life and
shows not half
your parts.

If I could write
the beauty of
your eyes

And in fresh
numbers
number all your
graces,

The age to
come would
say 'This poet
lies:

Such heavenly
touches ne'er
touch'd earthly
faces.'

So should my
papers yellow'd
with their age
Be scorn’d like
‘old men of less
truth than
tongue,

And your true
rights be term'd
a poet's rage
And stretched
metre of an
antique song:
But were some
child of yours
alive that

time,

You should live
twice; in it and
in my thyme.

XVI.

Shall I compare
thee toa
summer's day?
Thou art more
lovely and
more
temperate:
Rough winds
do shake the
darling buds of
May,

And summer's
lease hath all
too short a date:

sometime

loved I view in
thee,

And thou, all
they, hast all
the all of me.

XXXIL

If thou survive
my well-
contented day,
When that
churl Death my
bones with dust
shall cover,
And shalt by
fortune once
more re-survey
These poor
rude lines of
thy deceased
lover,
Compare them
‘with the
bettering of the

*
ume,
And though
they be
outstripp'd by
every pen,
Reserve them
for my love,
not for their
thyme,
Exceeded by
the height of
happier men.
O, then
vouchsafe me
but this loving
thought:

"Had my
friend's Muse
grown with this
growing age,
A dearer birth
than this his
love had
brought,

To march in
ranks of better
equipage:

But since he
died and poets
better prove,
Theirs for their
style I'll read,
his for his
Jove.’

XXXHI.

Full many a
glorious
morning have I
seen

Flatter the
‘mountain-tops
‘with sovereign
eye,

Kissing with
golden face the
meadows
green,

Gilding pale
streams with
heavenly
alchemy;
Anon permit
the basest
clouds to ride
With ugly rack
on his celestial

face,
And from the

To ‘cide this
title is
impanneled

A quest of
thoughts, all
tenants to the
heart,

And by their
verdict is
determined

The clear eye's
moiety and the
dear heart's part:
As thus; mine
eye's due is thy
outward part,
And my heart's
right thy inward
love of heart.

XLVII.

Betwixt mine
eye and heart a
league is took,
And each doth
good turns now
unto the other:
When that mine
eye is famish'd
for a look,

Or heart in love
with sighs
himself doth
smother,

With my love's
picture then my
eye doth feast
And to the
painted banquet
bids my heart;
Another

{EME mine
eye is my
heart's guest
And in his
thoughts of love
doth share a

part:
So, either by thy
picture or my
love,

Thyself away
art resent still
with me;

For thou not
farther than my
thoughts canst
move,

And I am still
with them and
they with thee;
Or, if they
sleep, thy
picture in my
sight

Awakes my
heart to heart's
and eye's
delight.

XLVI.

How careful
was I, when I
took my way,
Each trifle
under truest
bars to thrust,
That to my use
it might unused
stay

From hands of
falsehood, in
sure wards of

not so great:

It is my love
that keeps mine
eye awake;
Mine own true
love that doth
my rest defeat,
To play the
watchman ever
for thy sake:
For thee watch
1 whilst thou
dost wake
elsewhere,
From me far
off, with others
all too near.

LX.

Sin of self-love
possesseth all
mine eye

And all my soul
and all my
every part;

And for this sin
there is no
remedy,

Itis so
grounded
inward in my
heart.

Methinks no
face so gracious
is as mine,

No shape so
true, no truth of
such account;
And for myself
mine own
‘worth do
define,

As J all other in
all worths
surmount.

But when my
glass shows me
myself indeed,
Beated and
chopp'd with
tann'd antiquity,
Mine own self-
love quite
contrary I read;
Self so self-
loving were
iniquity.

‘Tis thee,
myself, that for
myself I praise,
Painting my
age with beauty
of thy days.

LXIL

Against my
love shall be, as
Tam now,
With

*
times
injurious hand
crush'd and
o'er-worn;
When hours
have drain'd his
blood and fill'd
his brow
With lines and
wrinkles; when
his youthful
morn
Hath travell'd
‘on to age's

learning
mayst thou
taste.
‘The wrinkles
which thy
glass will
truly show
Of mouthed
graves will
give thee
memory;
Thou by thy
dial's shady
stealth mayst
know

a
times
thievish
progress to
eternity.
Look, what
thy memory
can not
contain
Commit to
these waste
blanks, and
thou shalt find
Those
children
nursed,
deliver'd from
thy brain,
To take anew
acquaintance
of thy mind.
These offices,
so oft as thou
wilt Look,
Shall profit
thee and
much enrich
thy book.

LXXVIU.

So oft have I
invoked thee
for my Muse
And found
such fair
assistance in
my verse

As every alien
pen hath got
my use

And under
thee their
poesy
disperse.
Thine eyes
that taught the
dumb on high
to sing

And heavy
ignorance
aloft to fly
Have added
feathers to the
learned's wing
And given
grace a
double
majesty,

Yet be most
proud of that
which I
compile,
Whose
influence is
thine and born
of thee:

In others!
works thou
dost but mend
the style,

‘Of more delight
‘than hawks or
horses be;

And having
thee, of all
men's pride I
boast:
Wretched in
this alone, that
thou mayst take
All this away
and me most
wretched make.

XCIL

But do thy
worst to steal
thyself away,
For term of life
thou art assured
mine,

And life no
longer than thy
love will stay,
For it depends
upon that love
of thine.

Then need I not
to fear the
worst of.
wrongs,

When in the
least of them
my life hath
end.

T see a better
state to me
belongs

‘Than that
which on thy
humour doth
depend;

Thou canst not
vex me with
inconstant
mind,

Since that my
life on thy
revolt doth lie.
O, what a
happy title do I
find,

Happy to have
thy love, happy
to die!

But what's so
blessed-fair
that fears no
blot?

Thou mayst be
false, and yet I
know it not.

CIT.

So shall I live,
supposing thou
art true,

Like a deceived
husband; so
Jove's face
May still seem
love to me,
though alter'd
new;

Thy looks with
me, thy heart in
other place:
For there can
live no hatred
in thine eye,
Therefore in
that I cannot
know thy
change.

cvil.

‘Not mine own

Of the wide

on things to

Can yet the

control,
Supposed as

The mortal

And the sad

Incertainties

assured
And peace

olives of

My love looks

Since, spite of

And thou in

When tyrants’

Which hath not
figured to thee
my true spirit?

Shakespeare's Sonnets

For shame!
deny that thou
bear'st love to
any,
Who for
thyself art so
‘unprovident.
Grant, if thou
wilt, thou art
beloved of
many,
But that thou
none lovest is
most evident;
For thou art so
possess'd with
murderous hate
‘That 'gainst
thyself thou
stick’st not to
conspire.
Seeking that
beauteous roof
to ruinate
Which to repair
should be thy
chief desire.
O, change thy
thought, that I
‘may change
my mind!
Shall hate be
} fairer lodged
than gentle
love?
Be, as thy
presence is,
gracious and
kind,
Or to thyself at
least kind-
hearted prove:
Make thee
another self,
for love of me,
That beauty
still may live in
thine or thee.

XI.

As fast as thou
shalt wane, so
fast thou
growest

In one of thine,
from that
which thou
departest;

And that fresh
blood which
youngly thou
bestowest
Thou mayst
call thine when
thou from
youth
convertest.
Herein lives
wisdom,
‘beauty and
‘increase:
Without this,
folly, age and
cold decay:

Tf all were
minded so, the

times
should cease

Are windows to
my breast,
‘where-through
the sun
Delights to
peep, to gaze
‘therein on thee;
Yet eyes this
cunning want to
grace their art;
‘They draw but
‘what they see,
know not the
heart.

XXV.

Let those who
are in favour
with their stars
Of public
honour and
proud titles
‘boast,

‘Whilst I, whom
fortune of such
triumph bars,
Unlook'd for
joy in that I
honour most.
Great princes’
favourites their
fair leaves
spread

But as the
‘marigold at the
sun's eye,

And in
themselves
their pride lies
buried,

For at a frown
they in their
glory die.

The painful
warrior
famoused for
fight,

After a
thousand
victories once
foil'd,

Is from the
book of honour
razed quite,
And all the rest
forgot for
which he toil'd:
Then happy I,
that love and
am beloved
Where I may
not remove nor
be removed.

XXXVI.

Lord of my
love, to whom
in vassalage
Thy merit hath
‘my duty
strongly knit,
To thee I send
this written
embassage,
To witness
duty, not to
show my wit:
Duty so great,
which wit so
poor as mine
May make
seem bare, in
‘wanting words
to show it,

XL.

Take all my
loves, my love,
yea, take them
all;

What hast thou
then more than
thou hadst
before?

No love, my
love, that thou
mayst true love
All mine was
thine before
thou hadst this
more.

Then if for my
love thou my
love receivest,
I cannot blame
thee for my
Jove thou usest;
But yet be
blamed, if thou
thyself
deceivest

By wilful taste
of what thyself
refusest.

I do forgive thy
tobbery, gentle
thief,

Although thou
steal thee all
my poverty;
And yet, love
knows, it is a
greater grief
To bear love's
wrong than
hate's known
injury.
Lascivious
grace, in whom
all ill well
shows,

Kill me with
spites; yet we
must not be
foes.

LV.

Not marble, nor

Than unswept

°

bum

You live in this,

Sweet love,
tenew thy force;
be it not said
Thy edge

be than appetite,
Which but to-

To-morrow

Thy hungry

To-morrow see

My tongue-

Me for my

‘And threescore

XXVI.

Weary with

to my bed,

The dear repose
for limbs with
travel tired;

But then begins
ajourney in my
head,

To work my
mind, when
body's work's
expired:

For then my

far where I

Intend a

And keep my

Looking on

Which, like a

Makes black
night beauteous
and her old face
new.

Lo! thus, by

by night my
mind,

For thee and for
myself no quiet
find.

_

Following the pattern of one use of the word ‘time’ for every 1 4 sonnets there should be at least 17
instances of 'time' utilized in the last 26 sonnets. For it not to appear even once is proof that at least
one other person wrote those last 26 sonnets.*

At the time the sonnets were initially published there were no copywrite laws and almost anything
was allowed. The first known publishing was in 1609 which was at least 10 years or more after they
were written. They could have been published prior to this date but that is unknown. However, it
would explain why someone added 26 sonnets. By including the additional 26 sonnets it would have

been seen as a 17% century version of the ‘directors cut version of a film' and thereby allowed the
1609 publisher to sell the most books that he possibly could.

*Utilization of words is a good way to determine an author. A person will typically utilize the same
word. One person will use the word ‘daytime’ while others will use the word ‘dawn' or even ‘day’ in
place of it. For example these are all the same question:: 'Is it daytime yet? Is it dawn yet?’ and 'Has
the day dawned?! People will use the same word(s) almost every time.

I used the word 'time' as my example for an additional reason. In early plays the concept of time is
only projected by the bard.

Thinking about events in the past or future expands our thinking. You can say that it adds a fourth
dimension to our thinking but it actually uses more parts of our brain than our normal here and now

file://C:\Documents%20and%20Settings aaa _ocal%20Settings\Temporary%2... 05/03/2007
; | Shakespeare's Sonnets. Plain text 1-154. Page 14 of 15

ruc process uses, It's a large part of fantasy but more specific it loosens the boundaries of our
minds and inspires us all to think creatively.

When we hear the word 'time' we switch on that whole section of our brain in anticipation of using it.
Whether we use that part or not our mind automatically enters that realm of fantasy. It's a Pavlovian
type reaction and it works in less than half a second. Faster than a bell makes one of Pavlov's dogs
salivate.

A similar effect which is perhaps easier to explain was the start of each show of ‘Star Trek The Next
Generation’ which began with the word 'space’.

"Space... The final frontier... These are the voyages of the Starship Enterprise. Its continuing
mission: To explore strange new worlds... To seek out new life; new civilisations... To boldly go
where no one has gone before!"

The word space causes you to suddenly think of the universe which expands your thinking in
preparation for the show . The bard used the word ‘stars’ about 100 times in the plays to achieve a
similar 'spacing out' effect. However, it was included in most romantic scenes as a way to increase
the reach of their minds. Just read Romeo's opening lines of the balcony scene in Romeo and Juliet
and see how much the words stars, heaven, sun and moon set up the whole fantasy or romance of
that scene.

ROMEO

He jests at scars that never felt a wound.

But, soft! what light through yonder window breaks?
It is the east, and Juliet is the sun.

Arise, fair sun, and kill the envious moon,
Who is already sick and pale with grief,

That thou her maid art far more fair than she:
Be not her maid, since she is envious;

Her vestal livery is but sick and green

And none but fools do wear it; cast it off.

It is my lady, O, it is my love!

O, that she knew she were!

She speaks yet she says nothing: what of that?

Shakespeare's Sonnets. Plain text 1-154.

@. eye discourses; I will answer it.
I am too bold, 'tis not to me she speaks:
Two of the fairest stars in all the heaven,
Having some business, do entreat her eyes
To twinkle in their spheres till they return.
What if her eyes were there, they in her head?
The brightness of her cheek would shame those stars,
As daylight doth a lamp; her eyes in heaven
Would through the airy region stream so bright
That birds would sing and think it were not night.
See, how she leans her cheek upon her hand!
O, that I were a glove upon that hand,

That I might touch that cheek!

Concerning UFO's:
What are the UFO's sightings about?

I am almost certain they are mainly to serve large dishes of humble pie. It had it's
greatest effect when Ronald Reagan was very close to declaring Nuclear war with the
Soviet Union in the early 1980's.

His logic went something like this. Nobody is greater than us Americans. We have the
worlds fastest jets and the most advanced radar network which makes us invulnerable.
It was obvious to him that we would win a nuclear war with Russia.

Except that UFO's are much faster, disappear and reappear at will on our best radar
and abduct us whenever they want. Maybe we are not really that great. So we better not
start a Nuclear War.

Besides that we might need Soviets on our side if UFO's invade us.
Remember that strange speech of President Reagans?

<11/19/85 - President Reagan and Soviet General Secretary Gorbachev meet at their
first Summit in Geneva, Switzerland. It was during those private discussions where
Reagan stated, " I couldn't help but say to him, just think how easy his task and
mine might be in these meetings that we held if suddenly there was a threat to this

Who was the Pearly Queen?

This is Tate Gallery's 'Portrait of an
Unknown Lady' by Marcus Gheeraerts
ll and she is called the 'Pearly Queen’.
4/pearlyqueen.htm>

But who was she and what was her
name?

She was this woman: This is Marcus
Gheeraerts painting of 'Anne, Lady Pope
with her children’ at the National Gallery
>http:/Awww.npg.org.uk/live/search/portra
it-asp?mkey=mw69849<

It's easy to see to see that she was the
same person. She has changed her
collar and removed her apron of fabric
but she is still the same person..

I'll explain all the differences and at the
same time prove that they are the same
woman five ways.

Qne: Look at the dark part of her bodice which frames her chest. Look at the shape, color
and how it fits. It is identical on both paintings even so far as how it spreads out to
encompass the back of her shoulders. That is very unique.

Two: The color of the dress has faded differently in the two paintings but they look identical
as far as the style and shape. They both seem to have the same or similar ornate design
with an intricate pattern which was uncommon then. Solids colors or simple designs were
much more favored then. Particularly uncommon was the 'Pearly Queen's' paisley design
which was imported from the Safavid Dynasty of Persia. Yes, it is true that the design does
not appear to be exactly the same in the painting 'Anne, Lady Pope with her children’ but |
resolve this inconsistency next.

Three: Likely the painting 'Anne, Lady Pope with her children’ is what was called a ‘second!
which was a painting that an artist often provided for free. A second was hung in the main
hall or room where it could be viewed by many people but where it would be subject to a
herd of small children, animals and smoke from the large fireplaces. The primary painting
would be hung in a bedroom or library where it was safe from destructive forces. Usually the
second was a quickly made copy of the primary painting but not always and this appears to
be one of those exceptions.

What would show that 'Anne, Lady Pope with her children' was a 'second'? The detail is
lacking. The proof usually that the brush strokes are broader since they only put about 10%
of the time into making seconds (but | can't see the paint strokes from here and it costs
about $800 for a plane ticket to London from here in Flagstaff, Arizona to compare them).
That is probably why there is less detail in the dress and the faces. That is also why the
colors are different. They used cheaper but more stable pigments in the seconds. It's a bit
ironic but the best paintings lost the most color. The old masters often used more exotic
organic pigments on their best paintings to get the perfect colors at that time but these would
often fade in time while the 'second' or cheaper paintings (and those by lesser artists) were
made with standard pigments which were less true as far as their color at the time but were
more stable. It appears that a slightly orangish yellow or gold pigment is what faded out
almost completely in the ‘Portrait of an Unknown Lady. Actually everything except the blue
seems to have faded quite a bit but all | am concerned with for this proof are the yellows.

All colors in a painting excepting flesh tones can be approximated with standard pigments.
Who is going to know if a green dress is slightly off color? However, a persons skin color
had to be very accurate since it would be noticed immediately if it wasn't true. So the
masters were always bent on using the best yellows and oranges that became available in
order to get the perfect flesh tones but like many 400 year old tapestries they too fade in
time.

In looking at the Pearly Queen | suddenly realized that her dark blue dress was just too dark.
Before then or later it would have been worn but such a drab color dress would not have
been appropriate during the era of Queen Elizabeth's colorful reign. Both her face and her
hair were darker than Lady Pope's.

So | took the Pearly Queen and increased the color saturation and balance using automatic
settings and then | shifted the blue/yellow balance towards the yellow region quite a bit and
the cyan/red a tiny bit towards the red using 'Paint Shop' (please forgive me for the horror of
performing this experiment on a great work of art) to match her hair color to that of Lady
Anne Pope and guess what?

She doesn't look like she is going
to die of anemia anymore does
she?

identical facial features as Robert Peake's
‘Lady Elizabeth Pope’ dated 1615 which is
